# 39 — Requirements Traceability Matrix

Status: GENERATED by `tools/traceability.py` — do not edit by hand. Regenerate after any specification change: `python3 tools/traceability.py`.

## 1. Purpose

Links every requirement: RESEARCH/FINDING (Evidence) → THREAT → REQUIREMENT → IMPLEMENTATION COMPONENT → TEST/VERIFICATION → DOCUMENTATION (defining spec file). The generator also lints the set: duplicate IDs, missing verification on security requirements (ERROR, release-blocking per 27-SECURE-DEVELOPMENT.md), missing evidence, dangling test/threat references.
